Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the nuclear_physics structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 760. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 566 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 760 + O(1/n^566). Summing over all modes and applying the nuclear_physics convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
